- 2026-08-23 — Real Auth.js wiring for member login (Prompt 2), replacing the `sessionStorage`-only mock from the two 2026-08-21 entries with a genuine second Credentials provider, a server-side `/account` gate, and a real Navbar session read — **`/account`'s displayed Profile/Membership/Bookings content is still `SAMPLE_*` data, unchanged; that's Prompt 3.** **`auth.ts`**: added a second `Credentials` provider (`id: 'member-credentials'`, `name: 'Member Credentials'`) alongside the existing admin one, which is untouched byte-for-byte apart from its own block (confirmed via `git diff`) — `authorize` looks up `Customer` by email, returns `null` if not found or `passwordHash` is still null (never activated) or `verifyPassword` fails (same no-field-hint convention as the admin provider), otherwise returns `{ id, name, email, role: 'member' }`. The existing `jwt`/`session` callbacks were already generic (no admin-specific DB round-trip) and needed no logic change — only their `as string` casts on `role` were adjusted to match the now-narrower type (see next-auth.d.ts below); this is a type-level fix only, not a behavior change, confirmed by the callback code being otherwise identical. **`src/types/next-auth.d.ts`**: `role` widened from `string` to the literal union `'admin' | 'member'` on `Session.user`, `User`, and `JWT` — deliberately not the Prisma `AdminRole` enum, which is conceptually admin-only. A repo-wide grep found zero existing `session.user.role === '...'` comparisons anywhere, so no other file depended on the old wider `string` type. **`src/app/layout.tsx`**: wrapped `{children}` in `<SessionProvider>` from `next-auth/react`, rendered directly from the server-component root layout (no `'use client'` needed on the layout itself) so `useSession()` is available anywhere in the tree. **`/login`**: `src/components/auth/LoginForm.tsx` was restructured from a `'use client'` component doing a hardcoded `DUMMY_CREDENTIALS` comparison + `sessionStorage.setItem` into a plain presentational component (`action`/`error` props, no client state, no `'use client'` — the old fake-credential logic is gone entirely, no fallback) mirroring `admin/login/page.tsx`'s split as closely as the two pages' different visual shells allow. `src/app/login/page.tsx` gained a `memberSignIn` server action (`'use server'`, mirrors `admin/login/page.tsx`'s `authenticate` function line-for-line: `signIn('member-credentials', { email, password, redirectTo: '/account' })` inside a try/catch, `redirect('/login?error=1')` on `AuthError`) and reads `searchParams.error` to pass `error={error === '1'}` into `LoginForm`; the existing Navbar/hero/Footer shell and "Member Access" eyebrow are untouched. The error copy reused admin's exact "Invalid email or password." string verbatim (a judgment call — it's generic, not admin-specific wording, and it's also the same text the old fake `LoginForm` already showed, so the visible copy is unchanged even though the mechanism behind it is now real). **`/account`**: `src/app/account/page.tsx` converted from a `'use client'` component polling `sessionStorage` in a `useEffect` into an `async` server component calling `const session = await auth()`, redirecting to `/login` via `redirect()` unless `session?.user?.id` and `session.user.role === 'member'` are both true — a real, self-sufficient check that doesn't depend on `middleware.ts` (which CLAUDE.md already documents as non-executing in this dev setup, and whose admin-only matcher was not touched or extended here). `AccountProfile`/`MembershipStatusCard`/`RecentBookingsList` render exactly as before, still fed `SAMPLE_*` data — this prompt deliberately does not thread real session data into them. **Navbar**: `src/components/layout/Navbar.tsx` swapped its `sessionStorage`-polling `useEffect` for `const { data: session, status } = useSession()`; `signedIn = status === 'authenticated' && session?.user?.role === 'member'` naturally covers the "no flash of a logged-in badge while `status === 'loading'`" requirement, since `loading` isn't `'authenticated'`. Both the desktop and mobile initials badges now derive from `getInitials(session?.user?.name ?? '')` instead of `SAMPLE_PROFILE.name`; the now-unused `SAMPLE_PROFILE` import was removed (confirmed `getInitials` is the only remaining import from `AccountProfile.tsx`, via grep). Sign Out now calls `signOut({ callbackUrl: '/' })` from `next-auth/react` instead of clearing `sessionStorage` + `router.push` — the now-unused `useRouter` import/`router` variable were removed too, since nothing else in the file used it. No new npm dependency (`next-auth/react` ships with the already-installed `next-auth@5` package) — `package.json`/`package-lock.json` diff confirmed empty. **VERIFY**: admin regression — `auth.ts`'s admin provider block confirmed byte-identical via `git diff` (the only changes inside the `callbacks` block are the two `role` cast adjustments described above); `/admin` with no session still redirects to `/admin/login` (307); `/api/auth/providers` lists both `credentials` and `member-credentials` correctly. Member sign-in end-to-end, no passwords typed into any browser form: a disposable `Customer` was created directly via a throwaway script with a real `hashPassword`-generated `passwordHash` (simpler than re-running the full approval→activation-email→`/api/activate` chain, which Prompt 1's own 2026-08-22 VERIFY already covered end-to-end) — `GET /api/auth/csrf` → `POST /api/auth/callback/member-credentials` (csrfToken + email + password + cookie jar, via `curl`) → `GET /api/auth/session` returned exactly `{"user":{"name":"Verify Member Login","email":"verify-member-login@example.com","id":"...","role":"member"},"expires":"..."}`. `/account` gate: no-cookie request → `307` to `/login`; same request with the valid session cookie → `200`. Navbar, in the Browser pane: the real `authjs.session-token` JWE cookie was injected via `document.cookie` (not by typing a password — it's `HttpOnly` by default, but no such cookie pre-existed in this fresh browser context, and per spec JS may still set a *new* cookie of that name when none already carries the `HttpOnly` flag; the running dev server decrypts/validates it identically either way since the token is self-contained) — reloading `/` showed the "VM" initials badge (matching "Verify Member Login") + "Sign Out", not "Book a Court"/"Sign In"; clicking Sign Out redirected to `/`, reverted Navbar to the logged-out state, cleared `document.cookie` to empty, and a subsequent navigation to `/account` in the same browser context redirected to `/login`. Regression: `/`, `/book`, `/membership`, `/membership/apply`, `/cafe-bar`, `/about`, `/news` all returned `200` logged-out (`curl`); `/book` and `/membership` were also reloaded in the Browser pane both logged-out and with the injected session, zero new console errors either way; `/account` itself was loaded with the injected session and confirmed to render all three sections (still sample data) with the real "VM"/Sign Out Navbar state layered on top, zero console errors. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (two `role` cast fixes in `auth.ts` were needed to get there — see above). `package.json`/`package-lock.json` diff empty. **Cleanup**: the disposable `Customer` row and both throwaway setup/cleanup scripts were deleted after the VERIFY pass; `git status --short` confirmed only the 7 intended files remain modified. **CRITICAL, out-of-scope-to-fix-here, flagging for immediate follow-up**: this prompt's own VERIFY surfaced that a `role: 'member'` session now passes every existing admin gate, because none of them check `role` — only that a session exists. Confirmed live: `GET /admin` with the member session cookie returned `200` (not a redirect to `/admin/login`), and `POST /api/admin/resources` with the same cookie returned `400` (a validation error on the request body) rather than the `401` an unauthenticated request gets — meaning it passed the route's `if (!session?.user?.id)` check entirely. This is a direct consequence of `(protected)/layout.tsx` and every `/api/admin/*` route's own check (`!session` / `!session?.user?.id`) never having needed to check `role`, back when `AdminUser` was the only kind of session that could exist — adding the member provider silently invalidated that assumption. This prompt's explicit scope forbade touching any admin route or `middleware.ts` to keep the admin regression diff clean, so it was deliberately **not fixed** here, but it means any activated member can currently reach the full admin panel and its mutating API routes. **This should be treated as a blocking follow-up, not a someday item** — before any promotion to `staging`/`main`, every admin gate (`(protected)/layout.tsx` and each `/api/admin/*` route's own check) needs `session.user.role === 'admin'` added alongside its existing session-existence check, and `middleware.ts`'s own `auth()` wrapper likely needs the same even though it's currently dead code locally, in case it does execute on `staging`/Vercel.

- 2026-08-24 — Closed the critical admin-gate gap flagged by the 2026-08-23 entry's VERIFY: a `role: 'member'` session (introduced by that same prompt's second Credentials provider) was passing every existing admin auth check, since none of them checked `role` — only that a session existed. **Fix**: widened the session-existence condition to also require `role === 'admin'`, everywhere that condition already existed — no new shared helper introduced (confirmed via read that all 9 route files use the identical inline `if (!session?.user?.id) {...}` pattern, no shared auth-check function exists to fix once). `src/app/admin/(protected)/layout.tsx`'s `if (!session)` became `if (!session || session.user.role !== 'admin')`. Every file matching `src/app/api/admin/**/route.ts` (enumerated fresh via glob, not assumed from any prior list) got its own `if (!session?.user?.id) {...}` widened to `if (!session?.user?.id || session.user.role !== 'admin') {...}`, same 401 status and same `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` body every route already used — 9 files, 11 occurrences (2 each in `resources/[id]/route.ts` and `bulletin/[id]/route.ts` for their PATCH+DELETE handlers, 1 each in the other 7): `add-on-pricing-rules/[id]`, `bookings/[id]/reschedule`, `bulletin` (POST), `bulletin/[id]` (PATCH+DELETE), `guest-fee-rule/[id]`, `memberships/[id]`, `pricing-rules/[id]`, `resources` (POST), `resources/[id]` (PATCH+DELETE). No route's response shape, status code, or error message changed — the role check is a strictly additional condition on the existing check, not a restructure. `middleware.ts` was deliberately left untouched, per this prompt's own scope decision — it's confirmed non-executing in this dev setup (see CLAUDE.md), so a role-check addition there can't even be exercised locally; flagging it as a follow-up for defense-in-depth on `staging`/Vercel rather than changing it blind. **CLAUDE.md**: the Tech Stack table's Auth row, the Customer & auth model bullet, and the Admin/staff model bullet were all still describing the pre-2026-08-23 state (`Customer` "carries no auth fields", member Auth.js "not yet wired") — corrected to reflect that member login is live, `/account`'s gate is real, and only its displayed content (`SAMPLE_*`) is still pending; the Admin/staff model bullet's own duplicate explanation was trimmed to a one-line cross-reference back to the Customer & auth model bullet, and gained a new sentence documenting this prompt's `role === 'admin'` check addition. **VERIFY**: the 9-file glob enumeration matched exactly what was touched, confirmed via `git diff --stat` (9 route files + layout + CLAUDE.md, nothing extra, nothing skipped). - 2026-08-24 — `/account` wired to real `Booking`/`Membership` data, closing out the "still sample data" caveat from the 2026-08-23/2026-08-24 (admin-gate) entries above — the last piece of the member-account thread. **Membership selection** (`src/app/account/page.tsx`): queries `Membership` for a `status: 'active'`/`endDate >= now` row first (`orderBy: startDate desc`), falling back to the customer's most recent `Membership` of any status if none is currently valid; `isExpired` is always computed from `membership.endDate < now` at read time, never from the stored `status` column, since nothing in this codebase auto-flips `status` when a membership lapses (the same "derive from timestamp, not a stale flag" pattern the booking-hold-expiry logic already uses). A found membership's total-credit figure comes from a separate `MembershipCreditTransaction` lookup (`reason: 'activation'`, earliest `createdAt`), falling back to `creditBalanceCentavos` for both total and remaining if no such transaction exists (avoids a divide-by-zero on the progress bar). Both membership queries and the bookings query are pinned to `relationLoadStrategy: 'query'`, matching the project's existing convention now that `relationJoins` is enabled project-wide. **New shared helpers**: `formatMembershipTier(tier)` added to `src/lib/membership-pricing.ts` (three_month/six_month/twelve_month → "3-Month"/"6-Month"/"12-Month", same mapping as the admin memberships pages' own local `formatTier` — that local copy was deliberately left untouched, out of scope here) and `formatBookingDateTime(date)` added to `src/lib/format.ts` (`Intl.DateTimeFormat` in `Asia/Manila`, matching the exact sample format the old hardcoded booking dates used, e.g. "Sep 12, 2026, 6:00 PM"). **Component conversions**: `AccountProfile.tsx`, `MembershipStatusCard.tsx`, and `RecentBookingsList.tsx` all lost their `SAMPLE_*` constants and became pure presentational components (props in, no self-fetching, matching this project's direct-Prisma-in-server-component convention) — `MembershipStatusCard` now takes a discriminated-union prop (`membership: null` for the graceful "No active membership on file" empty state, or a full membership shape otherwise) plus a standalone `customerId` used to derive the QR placeholder value (`WSS-MEMBER-${customerId}`, still visibly commented as a placeholder pending the real encoding-scheme decision — just no longer identical for every member) and a new visually-distinct "Expired" pill shown alongside the tier pill when `isExpired` is true; `RecentBookingsList` now shows a plain "No bookings yet." message when its `bookings` prop is empty instead of a broken zero-page paginator, and otherwise renders unchanged (10-per-page, icon-per-resource-type, Previous/Next). All data fetching/shaping (including the DB→display mapping for bookings, capped at a recent 50 rows via `take: 50`) happens once in `src/app/account/page.tsx`; the greeting now uses the freshly-queried `Customer.name`'s first token instead of a hardcoded name. A `Customer` row not found for a valid member session (defensive — a session referencing a deleted customer) redirects to `/login`, same as an invalid-role session. **VERIFY**: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `package.json`/`package-lock.json` diff empty (confirmed via `git status --short prisma/` and root diff — no new dependency). Used disposable `Customer`/`MembershipApplication`/`Membership`/`MembershipCreditTransaction`/`Booking` rows created via a throwaway script (`prisma/verify-account-seed.ts`, deleted after running), signed in via the real Auth.js HTTP flow (`GET /api/auth/csrf` → `POST /api/auth/callback/member-credentials` → cookie jar via `curl`, no passwords typed into any browser form) — four disposable customers, each hit via `curl -b <cookie-jar> http://localhost:3000/account` and the embedded RSC payload inspected directly (more reliable than scraping rendered HTML for a server component's props): **Scenario A** (currently-valid six-month membership + its activation credit transaction + 12 bookings across all 3 seeded resource types — Tennis Court/Pickleball Court/Golf Simulator — split 9 confirmed/3 cancelled) showed `tierName: "6-Month"`, `activationCentavos: 600000` (₱6,000.00), `creditCentavos: 650000` (₱6,500.00 total grant), `remainingCreditCentavos: 420000` (₱4,200.00), `expiryDateLabel: "February 15, 2027"`, `isExpired: false` (no Expired badge), correct 12/9/3 booking counts, and a QR `data=` param keyed to that customer's real id. **Scenario B** (stored `status: 'active'` but `endDate` in the past — the specific bug this prompt's date-derived-expiry logic exists to catch) correctly showed `isExpired: true` despite the `active` stored status. **Scenario C** (an older expired three-month membership plus a newer currently-valid six-month one) correctly surfaced the newer one (`tierName: "6-Month"`, `expiryDateLabel: "January 1, 2027"`), not the older expired one. **Scenario D** (a real session, zero `Membership` rows, zero `Booking` rows) rendered `membership: null` → "No active membership on file" and "No bookings yet." with no crash. Profile fields (real name/email/phone) and the "Welcome back, Verify" greeting (first token of `Customer.name`) were confirmed correct for every scenario. **Regression**: `/`, `/book`, `/membership`, `/login` all returned `200` for a signed-in member (curl); `/admin` with the same member session cookie still `307`-redirected to `/admin/login`, confirming the prior prompt's admin-gate `role === 'admin'` fix is unaffected. **Browser-pane verification was not possible this session** — the session cookie is `HttpOnly` (can't be injected via `document.cookie`, unlike the 2026-08-23 entry's fresh-browser-context workaround — this browser context already carried a leftover valid `HttpOnly` session cookie from an earlier verification session, blocking any JS-based overwrite) and the pane also isn't compositing frames right now (`computer` screenshot timed out — the same non-compositing issue flagged in the "Browser-pane verification limitation, expanded" Next-Up item), and typing a real password into the rendered `/login` form is off-limits regardless — so this VERIFY relied entirely on direct HTTP requests against the real Auth.js session flow rather than a live browser click-through; worth a dedicated look once the Browser-pane compositing issue is resolved. **Cleanup**: all four disposable customers and their `MembershipApplication`/`Membership`/`MembershipCreditTransaction`/`Booking` rows were deleted via a throwaway cleanup script; both throwaway scripts (seed + cleanup) were deleted afterward; `git status --short prisma/` confirmed clean.

- 2026-08-26 — Fixed duplicate Prisma query logging + documented the local-dev DB connection decision, both dev-environment-only, no application-logic change. **Duplicate logging fix** (`src/lib/prisma.ts`): the `prisma.$on('query', ...)` listener registration previously sat outside the `globalForPrisma.prisma ?? new PrismaClient(...)` singleton guard, at module top level — since this module can re-execute during Next.js dev hot-reload/Turbopack recompilation without recreating the already-cached client, each re-execution attached an additional listener to the same long-lived client instance, causing every query to log twice (or more) with identical timings. Fixed by moving client construction and listener registration into one `createPrismaClient()` function, called only when the singleton doesn't already exist — the listener is now guaranteed to attach exactly once per client instance. **Local-dev connection-pooling documentation** (`.env.local` + CLAUDE.md): `.env.local`'s `DATABASE_URL` was changed outside this prompt (in an earlier, unlogged step) from the pooled transaction-mode connection (port 6543, `pgbouncer=true`) to the session-mode connection (port 5432, same value already used by `DIRECT_URL`), to eliminate the `DEALLOCATE ALL`/extra-round-trip tax PgBouncer transaction mode imposes on every query — already verified via live browser testing to cut `/admin/resources` from ~1.7s to ~800ms and `/admin/memberships` from ~1.4s to ~500ms app-code time on a warm load. This prompt only documents that already-made, already-verified decision as locked-in: added an explanatory comment directly above `DATABASE_URL` in `.env.local` (value itself untouched), and a new Architecture Decisions bullet in CLAUDE.md making explicit that deployed environments (staging/production) must keep the pooled `:6543` connection — the split is deliberate, not an inconsistency to fix later. **VERIFY**: dev server restarted fully (env/module change, not a hot-reload-safe change); `/admin/resources` and `/admin/memberships` each reloaded in the Browser pane with at least 3 distinct queries per page spot-checked in the terminal log — each printed exactly once, no duplicates; no `DEALLOCATE ALL` lines appeared in either page's log, confirming the `:5432` connection is still active post-restart; `/admin/bookings` and `/admin/bulletin` reloaded as a regression check — both still work, query logs still print once each; admin login exercised end-to-end (session cookie set, redirect to `/admin`) to confirm the same client instance still works correctly under a different code path. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean — no type errors from the `prisma.ts` rewrite. `git status` confirmed only `src/lib/prisma.ts`, `.env.local`, `CLAUDE.md`, and `PROGRESS.md` were modified — no scope leakage. No test data created, no cleanup needed.

- 2026-08-27 — Member self-service password reset built end-to-end, closing out the "forgot-password is the next email case to build" thread. **Schema**: new `PasswordResetToken` model (`prisma/schema.prisma`), mirroring `MemberActivationToken`'s shape field-for-field (`customerId` FK, unique `tokenHash`, `expiresAt`, nullable `usedAt`) — hand-written migration `20260827020000_add_password_reset_token` (applied via `prisma migrate deploy`, the established shadow-DB-bypass convention), RLS enabled with the same `deny_all_anon`/`deny_all_authenticated` policy pair as every other table, added to both the migration and the master copy at `prisma/manual-sql/enable-rls-deny-all.sql`. **Lib**: new `src/lib/password-reset.ts` mirrors `member-activation.ts` exactly — `PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_HOURS` env var (default `1`, deliberately much shorter than activation's 48 hours, since this guards an existing account rather than onboarding a new one), `generatePasswordResetToken()`/`hashPasswordResetToken()` (SHA-256 over a 256-bit `crypto.randomBytes` token, no salt). **Email**: `sendPasswordResetEmail` added to `src/lib/resend.ts` as `buildBrandedEmail`'s third consumer (after `sendActivationEmail`/`sendRejectionEmail`) — eyebrow "PASSWORD RESET", heading "Reset Your Password", two short paragraphs (what happened, 1-hour expiry + safe-to-ignore notice) both ahead of the CTA, so it uses `buildBrandedEmail`'s own `ctaText`/`ctaUrl` params directly rather than the hand-rolled-button workaround `sendActivationEmail` needs — confirmed zero diff to `email-templates.ts`. **Routes**: `POST /api/auth/forgot-password` is enumeration-safe by design — looks up `Customer` by email, only creates a token + sends email if found and already activated (`passwordHash` set), but always returns the identical `200 { message: "If that email is registered, we've sent a password reset link." }` regardless of outcome (not found / never activated / found-and-emailed all indistinguishable). `POST /api/auth/reset-password` mirrors `/api/activate`'s validation order and one-`$transaction` update shape (set `passwordHash`, stamp `usedAt`), with a deliberately vaguer 404 ("Invalid or expired reset link") than activation's, since a reset link failing to resolve shouldn't hint at why. **Pages**: `/forgot-password` mirrors `/login`'s Navbar→hero→card→Footer shell (eyebrow "Password Reset", heading "Forgot Password"), single email field, generic confirmation state that never branches on found/not-found since the API itself never does. `/reset-password` mirrors `/activate`'s Suspense-wrapped shell and `ActivateForm`'s state machine — missing-token state, password+confirm fields, branches on the server's exact 400/404 messages, success state linking to `/login`. **Login wiring**: `LoginForm.tsx`'s static `<span>Forgot password?</span>` is now a real `<Link href="/forgot-password">`, same visual classes, no new hover state added (no existing sibling link in this file established one to follow). New untracked local-only `scripts/preview-password-reset-email.ts` (same structure as the two existing preview scripts — fixed sample token, `[PREVIEW]`-prefixed subject, `to: arjayrafaelical@gmail.com`, no Prisma import/DB writes); `.gitignore`'s bare `scripts/` entry already covers it (confirmed via `git check-ignore -v`), no `.gitignore` change needed. **VERIFY**: preview script confirmed via the Resend MCP — a `delivered` send with the `[PREVIEW]` subject and every element (eyebrow, heading, both paragraphs, CTA button text) present verbatim in HTML and plain text. Real end-to-end pass, same disposable-data/no-browser-password convention as every prior VERIFY: a disposable activated `Customer` (real `passwordHash` via a throwaway script) plus a disposable never-activated `Customer` were created. `POST /api/auth/forgot-password` against the activated customer's email returned the generic `200` and created exactly one `PasswordResetToken` row (`expiresAt` ~1 hour out, null `usedAt`) — the first attempt used a disposable `@example.com` address, which Resend's test-mode correctly rejected with a 422 `validation_error` (expected platform behavior, not a bug — `sendPasswordResetEmail`'s never-throws design swallowed it as intended and the route still returned the generic 200); the customer's email was then updated to a real deliverable address (`arjayrafaelical+pwreset@gmail.com`) and the request re-run, producing an actual `delivered` Resend send whose logged request body confirmed the emailed link pointed at `/reset-password?token=<the real raw token>`. Enumeration-safety confirmed for both an email that doesn't exist anywhere (no token row, no Resend send) and a real but never-activated `Customer` email (same). `POST /api/auth/reset-password` happy path with the real raw token and a matching ≥8-char password returned `200`; confirmed via the Supabase MCP that `Customer.passwordHash` changed and the token's `usedAt` was stamped; confirmed the customer could sign in with the new password via the real Auth.js HTTP flow (`GET /api/auth/csrf` → `POST /api/auth/callback/member-credentials`, cookie jar via `curl`) — a real session cookie came back on a `302`. All six failure paths confirmed: the now-used token reused → 400 "already been used"; a second token backdated via a throwaway script → 400 "has expired"; mismatched passwords → 400; a fabricated token → 404 "Invalid or expired reset link"; a 5-character password → 400 "at least 8 characters". Browser pane: `/forgot-password` renders the Navbar/hero/form shell with zero app-level console errors (only the long-standing HMR-websocket dev-environment noise, unrelated); `/reset-password` with no `?token=` shows the missing-token state with no `/api/auth/*` fetch attempted (confirmed via network-request log — only the Navbar's own unrelated `/api/auth/session` calls present); confirmed via DOM query that `LoginForm`'s "Forgot password?" is now a real `<a href="/forgot-password">`, not a `<span>`. Regression: `/login`, `/activate`, `/account` (redirects to `/login` unauthenticated, as expected), `/admin` and `/admin/memberships` (both redirect to `/admin/login` unauthenticated, as expected) all reloaded with zero app-level console errors; `git diff --stat` confirmed `auth.ts`, `middleware.ts`, and every `/admin` route file are untouched, and `email-templates.ts` has zero diff. Re-ran both existing preview scripts (approval + rejection) — both sent successfully with no errors, and since neither `email-templates.ts` nor either of those two `resend.ts` functions was touched, their output is unchanged from before this pass. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean both mid-pass and after the final edit. **Cleanup**: both disposable `Customer` rows and both `PasswordResetToken` rows were deleted via a throwaway script; all five throwaway scripts (seed, email-fix, token-expiry, cleanup, plus the one committed intentionally as `preview-password-reset-email.ts`) accounted for — the four throwaway ones deleted afterward. Before/after row counts matched exactly (`customer` 5→7→5, `passwordResetToken` 0→2→0). Also noted in passing: the dev server had to be stopped once mid-session to release a Windows file lock on the Prisma query-engine DLL before `npx prisma generate` could run — restarted afterward via the Browser-pane preview tool, not a code change.

The remaining light section below (now containing only `<ResetPasswordForm />`) had its own `bg-background` swapped for the real `bg-brand-light` token; the Suspense fallback's `text-neutral-700` was also swapped to `text-brand-dark/70` so the file has zero legacy/non-token classes left, not just the two called out in the prompt. Only `src/app/reset-password/page.tsx` touched — `Navbar.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `ResetPasswordForm.tsx`, and `forgot-password/page.tsx` untouched (the latter's lower section has this exact same `bg-background` bug — see Next Up). **VERIFY**: grep for `bg-background`/`text-neutral-700` in the file returned zero matches; `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. Browser-pane check (via computed styles/classNames, not screenshots — this session's pane wasn't compositing frames, consistent with the documented Browser-pane limitation below) confirmed at scroll 0 the `<header>`'s background is transparent and its nav-link color resolves to a light `oklab`/`lab` value (the existing scroll-conditional light-text branch), now sitting on the new `rgb(75, 46, 43)` (`#4B2E2B`, brand-dark) hero `<section>` behind it — legible, where before it sat on `bg-background`'s `#FFF8F0` (near-white) and was not; a dispatched `scroll` event past 100px confirmed the header's className switches to `bg-brand-light` exactly as on every other page (no regression to the existing scroll-transition behavior, which this prompt didn't touch). Full form flow re-verified end-to-end with a disposable `Customer`/`PasswordResetToken` pair (throwaway script, deleted after): submitting a new password returned the "Your password has been reset" success state with a working Sign In link, zero console errors. Mobile (375px): `document.documentElement.scrollWidth === clientWidth` (375 = 375), no horizontal overflow. Regression: `/forgot-password`, `/login`, `/activate`, `/account` all reloaded with zero console errors. `git status` confirmed only `src/app/reset-password/page.tsx` changed; two throwaway Prisma scripts used for the disposable token were deleted afterward, and the disposable `Customer`/`PasswordResetToken` rows were deleted via a cleanup script — no residual test data.
  - **Known follow-up, not fixed here**: `forgot-password/page.tsx`'s lower section has the identical `bg-background` legacy-token bug (its hero already has `bg-brand-dark`, so it isn't Navbar-invisibility-affected, but the light section below it is still on the wrong token) — flagged for its own follow-up prompt, out of scope for this single-file change.

- 2026-08-27 — Resource Add/Delete removed from the admin panel; replaced with a Disable/Enable toggle. New `Resource.disabledReason` column (nullable, cleared server-side whenever `isActive` flips back to `true`, regardless of request body). `POST /api/admin/resources` and `DELETE /api/admin/resources/[id]` deleted; `PATCH /api/admin/resources/[id]` extended to accept `isActive`/`disabledReason`. `ResourceFormModal` deleted (no longer used); new `DisableResourceModal` (reuses the shared `Modal`) prompts for an optional reason on disable. Disabled resources stay visible in the admin list (with their reason shown inline) but are now rejected server-side, not just UI-hidden: `POST /api/bookings` and `GET /api/availability` both 400 `{ error: 'Resource not found' }` for an inactive resource id, matching the not-found response shape so a disabled resource isn't distinguishable from a nonexistent one. New courts/simulators are now DB-injected only — no in-app create path.

- 2026-08-27 — Admin Pricing/Add-on rows gained full create/delete: `POST /api/admin/pricing-rules` and `POST /api/admin/add-on-pricing-rules` (409 on duplicate unique-combination, checked explicitly rather than relying on a raw Prisma P2002); `DELETE` added to both `[id]` routes (`PricingRule` hard-deletes with no referential check; `AddOnPricingRule` 409s with a friendly message if any `BookingAddOn` still references it). `PriceEditModal` gained a create mode (single-field POST form) alongside its existing multi-field edit/PATCH mode. Every previously-inert control in `ResourcesTabs` (disabled "+Add", disabled edit pencil) is now live — each price cell shows either a value with edit+delete, or a working "+Add"; the disabled-pencil placeholder was removed outright rather than kept as an inert state. Guest Fee confirmed staying edit-only, permanently, by design (its shape has nothing to key a second row on) — not a placeholder awaiting a future pass.

- 2026-08-27 — Full membership test-data reset #2, this time including Storage image cleanup — closes out the orphaned-gov-ID-images item from the 2026-08-27 reset #1, and clears everything created since (application, membership, credit ledger, activation token, password reset tokens). `Customer` rows preserved, `passwordHash` re-nulled. New `scripts/reset-membership-data.ts` (gitignored, standalone). Blocked mid-run by the local session-mode pooler maxing out at 15 idle connections unrelated to any live process; cleared with Arjay's explicit one-off approval to run `pg_terminate_backend` on the idle Supavisor sessions via the (normally read-only-scoped) Supabase MCP.

- 2026-08-27 — Audited all `scripts/` for Prisma connection cleanup: the 4 preview-*-email scripts are Resend-only (raw `fetch`, no Prisma import), confirmed untouched. `reset-membership-data.ts` was the only Prisma consumer, and its existing `.catch().finally(() => prisma.$disconnect())` chain had a real gap — the `catch` called `process.exit(1)` synchronously, which killed the process before `.finally()` ever ran, so a failed reset never disconnected. Fixed by swapping to `process.exitCode = 1`, letting the process exit naturally after cleanup runs on both paths. Closes out the idle-connection note from the 2026-08-27 membership-reset entry above.

- 2026-08-27 — `Bulletin` extended with richer optional metadata: 2 new `BulletinCategory` values (General, FacilityMaintenance — 4 originals unchanged), new `BulletinPriority` enum (Normal/High, default Normal), and 8 new nullable columns (affectedFacility, impact, action, eventStartAt/eventEndAt, expiresAt, ctaLabel/ctaUrl). Kept the existing `body` column rather than renaming it — only its admin-form label changed to "Description" — to avoid an unnecessary column rename on a live table. Admin Add/Edit modal and both admin API routes updated to match, with CTA label/URL enforced as a provided-together-or-neither pair (same pattern as the existing social platform/URL fields). Status: schema + admin modal done; `/news` and `AnnouncementGate` still render only the pre-existing fields — wiring the new fields into those surfaces (including `expiresAt` filtering) is not yet done. Required one out-of-scope type-only touch: `NewsGrid.tsx`'s `NewsCategory` union type had to widen to include the 2 new enum values so `/news/page.tsx` would still compile, since `NewsCard` displays `item.category` as plain text with no per-category branching.

- 2026-08-27 — New Bulletin fields wired into `/news` and `AnnouncementGate`. Shared `src/lib/bulletin.ts` (`bulletinNotExpiredWhere`/`bulletinOrderBy`) is now imported by both the `/news` query and `GET /api/bulletin/gate-notices` so expiry filtering (`expiresAt IS NULL OR expiresAt > now()`) and sort order (priority desc, then `publishedAt` desc) can't drift between the two — admin's own Bulletin list query is untouched, so staff still see expired rows there. `/news` (`NewsCard`) now renders the full field set: High-priority badge, "Affects: X" facility line, `body` as a labeled "Description" (replacing the old unlabeled excerpt paragraph), Impact/Action subsections, a formatted event-start/end line, and a CTA button — each omitted individually when its field is null. `AnnouncementGate` stays compact by design: priority badge plus a single "Facility — Action" line, with impact/event-window/CTA deliberately left off. No category filter UI existed on `/news` to extend with the 2 newer categories (Part 3.6 was a no-op, confirmed no such tabs/buttons exist). Verified live via Playwright: 6 fresh test bulletins (one per category, mixing High/Normal priority, past/future/null `expiresAt`, one full-field and one required-only) confirmed expired rows hidden on both public surfaces but still listed in `/admin/bulletin`, High-priority sorting first, full-field vs. required-only rendering both clean, and the pre-existing "Sample announcemint" plus the full `/book` wizard (SPORT step through resource list) unaffected — all 6 test rows deleted afterward via the admin panel.

- 2026-08-27 — Bulletin Add/Edit modal widened (384px → 768px, `Modal`'s shared `max-w-sm` default made an opt-in `maxWidthClassName` prop rather than changed globally, so every other consumer is untouched) and its compact fields regrouped into a responsive 2-column grid (Category/Priority, Event Start/End, Social Platform/URL, CTA Label/URL), collapsing to one column below `md`. Color/token styling of the admin form (cream vs. gray) remains an open, separately-flagged question — not addressed here. Follow-up pass, same modal, still uncommitted: native scrollbar replaced with a slim custom one (new `.scrollbar-thin` utility in `globals.css` — `scrollbar-width: thin`/`scrollbar-color` for Firefox, `::-webkit-scrollbar*` for Chromium/Safari — applied only to this form's own scroll container, so `PriceEditModal`/`DisableResourceModal`/other `Modal` consumers are untouched; confirmed via live regression screenshots against both); form's max-height raised 70vh → 80vh to cut scroll length; and the flat field list broken into 6 labeled sections (Content, Classification, Impact Details, Scheduling, Media & Links, Publish) via a small local `FormSection` wrapper, each divided by a thin `border-gray-100` rule with a `text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-500` label — reusing the same section-heading convention already used in `BookingsFilterModal`, no new colors/fonts. Field order/pairing from the prior pass untouched. Verified live: Add Bulletin renders all 6 sections with the new scrollbar top-to-bottom; a temporary test bulletin was created, edited, and saved successfully to confirm the new layout doesn't break submission, then deleted. Note: the pre-existing "Sample announcemint" bulletin referenced in earlier entries no longer exists in the local dev DB (table was empty at the start of this pass) — verification substituted a temporary bulletin instead. `tsc --noEmit` clean. Mobile-width (390px) resize verification was not completed — the connected browser session's window-resize tool had no effect on actual viewport width in this environment (window.innerWidth stayed at desktop size regardless of the requested resize), so the "sections stack cleanly on mobile" check is unverified live; no regression is expected since the responsive `grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2` classes are unchanged from the prior pass and the new section wrapper is a plain full-width `flex flex-col` block, but this should be spot-checked manually before considering the mobile pass fully verified.

- 2026-08-27 — 6 realistic sample bulletins seeded via new `scripts/seed-sample-bulletins.ts` (gitignored, standalone, mirrors `reset-membership-data.ts`'s Prisma-singleton/`.finally($disconnect)`/`process.exitCode` conventions), one per `BulletinCategory`, for visual review on `/news` and `/book` — persistent, not test data. `Bulletin.imageUrl` turned out to be a required column with no "no image" option, so all 6 use `/images/placeholder.jpg` (the same fallback path already named in `NewsGrid.tsx`'s own doc comment) rather than the originally-specified `null`. A pre-existing "Open Pickleball Tournament 2026" row from earlier manual admin testing collided by title with the Tournament sample and had already-expired/placeholder content; per Arjay's choice, the script overwrites (not skips) a title match, so re-running it always converges on the 6 spec bulletins rather than leaving stale duplicates.

- 2026-08-28 — Closes the `imageUrl`-required gap flagged in the entry above: `Bulletin.imageUrl` made nullable (hand-written migration `20260828010000_make_bulletin_image_url_nullable`, `prisma migrate deploy`), and per-category required-field rules added as data (`BULLETIN_CATEGORY_RULES` in new `src/lib/bulletin-validation.ts`) — image required only for Renovation/Tournament/Community, `eventEndAt`/`expiresAt` required for all except General, CTA pair required only for Tournament/Community; `affectedFacility`/`impact`/`action`/`eventStartAt` are now required unconditionally for every category (previously all-optional). Both admin API routes (`route.ts`/`[id]/route.ts`) had their duplicated `VALID_CATEGORIES`/parsing block extracted into that shared file so POST/PATCH can't drift, following the `booking-hold.ts`/`booking-pricing.ts` precedent. `BulletinFormModal`'s Category select moved to its own first section ahead of Content, with client-side validation mirroring the server and `*` markers (unconditional for the always-required fields, conditional via `BULLETIN_CATEGORY_RULES` for the rest). `BulletinList` and `NewsCard` now render cleanly with a null image (gray placeholder box in the admin list; `NewsCard` skips the image block entirely and relocates the category/priority badges into the card body so that information isn't lost). `PATCH` allows a category switch away from an image-required category to keep an existing image rather than forcing removal, since no removal affordance exists yet.

- 2026-08-28 — Admin Bulletin page layout fix: removed the italic "Add/Edit/Delete are live…" description line under the heading, and restructured `bulletin/page.tsx` to use the same fixed-header/scrollable-body pattern as Bookings/Memberships (`flex h-full flex-col` outer container, heading + Add button fixed outside a `min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-auto` wrapper) — previously the whole content card scrolled together as one block. Since the "+ Add Bulletin" button lived inside `BulletinList.tsx`'s own render output alongside the list rather than in the page, keeping it fixed required extracting it into a new small client component, `BulletinAddButton.tsx` (button + its own `addModalOpen` state + the existing `BulletinFormModal` in add mode); `BulletinList.tsx` now only renders the list and the edit modal. - 2026-08-28 — Admin Dashboard tab built, replacing the bare "Welcome to the admin panel" placeholder: 5 stat cards (Bookings Today, Revenue This Month, Pending Applications, Active Memberships, Resource Utilization), 3 `recharts` charts (stacked bar for the 14-day bookings trend, gradient area for the 6-month revenue trend, donut + written legend for bookings-by-resource-type), and two activity lists (Recent Bookings with neutral status pills, Pending Membership Applications with a "Review →" link to `/admin/memberships`). All new: `DashboardStatCard`/`DashboardStats`/`DashboardCharts`/`DashboardActivity` components, `dashboard-mock-data.ts`, two new `AdminIcons.tsx` glyphs (`RevenueIcon`/`TrendingIcon`, the latter unused for now — no obvious slot in the current 5-card/3-chart layout, kept for a future stat/chart that needs an up-trend glyph). Hardcoded mock data only in this pass, every export carrying a comment naming its future real Prisma source — no live queries added. Every chart/UI color is drawn from the existing neutral gray scale (`#111827`/`#374151`/`#6b7280`/`#9ca3af`/`#d1d5db`/`#e5e7eb`/white); no recharts default palette, no brand tokens, no color-coded up/down deltas. `recharts` added as a new approved dependency (see CLAUDE.md).

- 2026-08-28 — Member-aware booking flow shipped: `isActiveMember(customerId)` extracted from `customer-resolution.ts` as the shared source of truth; `/book` converted to an async server component (new `BookingPageClient.tsx` holds the prior client logic) that reads the session and builds a `memberContext` for a logged-in active member; `POST /api/bookings` is now session-aware — resolves `isMember` itself, prices at the member rate, attaches `customerId` directly at hold creation (no PATCH round-trip), and forces `guestCount` to 0 server-side for members regardless of what the client sends. `BookingForm` derives a `rateTier` from `memberContext` and threads it through duration options, ball-boy/coaching pricing, and the price estimate; `SportStep`'s pricing modal shows real member rates (confirmed live: tennis court ₱650/hr vs ₱750 non-member, golf-sim 30-min tier ₱450 appears only for members); `AddOnsStep` hides the guest-count control for members and shows a short explanatory line instead; `PaymentStep` skips the name/phone/email form for a known member, showing "Booking under {name} ({email})" instead. `MembershipStatusCard`'s "Book a Court" placeholder is now a real `Link` to `/book`. `PATCH /api/bookings/[id]` (the anonymous two-phase pricing/re-pricing path) is untouched and confirmed still working via VERIFY.

- 2026-08-28 — `/book/confirmation` moved onto brand tokens: was still on legacy Next-starter tokens (`bg-zinc`, `dark:` variants, `bg-foreground`/`text-background` on the Check Again button) across all 3 files, had no `Navbar`/`Footer` at all, and left the `cancelled`/`not_found` terminal states as dead ends. `BookingConfirmation.tsx` kept its own ledger (Part 1: `BookingSummary` reuse was assessed and rejected — its `isCourt` prop has no clean source in `BookingDetail`, since the API only returns a display `resource.typeName` like "Tennis Court", not the underlying `category` enum, and its footer copy is estimate-only phrasing that's wrong for an already-paid booking) restyled onto the identical `rounded-2xl border border-brand-dark/10 bg-brand-light shadow-xl shadow-brand-dark/10` card treatment, plus a "Back to Home" CTA. `ConfirmationContent.tsx`'s loading/stalled/cancelled/not-found copy and Check Again button moved to brand tokens; `cancelled` and `not_found` each gained a "Back to Home" text link so they're no longer dead ends. `page.tsx` gained `Navbar`/`Footer` plus the `/reset-password`/`/account` fix pattern for the invisible-Navbar-on-a-light-page bug (a `bg-brand-dark` header band directly below `<Navbar />`, not a padding-only patch) — documented as a reusable Known Issue in CLAUDE.md. `/book` itself (gate + wizard) deliberately left with the same unfixed bug — see Next Up.

- 2026-08-28 — `sendBookingConfirmationEmail` wired into the PayMongo webhook's `payment.paid` handler (`src/app/api/webhooks/paymongo/route.ts`) — customers now receive two emails on a paid booking (PayMongo's own generic receipt, plus this branded one). Fires only on the actual `pending_payment → confirmed` transition, guarded by the pre-existing `booking.payment?.status === 'paid'` early-return so a redelivered webhook event never double-sends. Booking lookup expanded to include `customer`/`resource.resourceType`/`addOns.addOnService`/`addOns.addOnPricingRule`; guest fee recomputed via `prisma.guestFeeRule.findFirst()` (mirrors `booking-pricing.ts`'s query), add-on display names built from a local `ADD_ON_EMAIL_LABELS` map with a raw-slug fallback and a "(N pax)" suffix when `addOnPricingRule.paxCount` is set. Email failures are swallowed internally by `sendBookingConfirmationEmail` itself, so the webhook still returns 200 to PayMongo either way.

Deletes only `BookingAddOn`/`Payment`/`BookingReschedule`/`Booking` rows (FK-safe children-first order, single transaction); `Customer`/`Membership`/every other model untouched. Also clears the 3 stray `pending_payment` test bookings flagged in the 2026-08-28 member-aware-booking VERIFY entry, plus every other test booking created across the recent SportStep/webhook-email/confirmation-page VERIFY passes. Console output: Booking 6→0, Payment 4→0, BookingAddOn 2→0, BookingReschedule 0→0; Customer (6) and Membership (1) unchanged. `/admin/bookings` confirmed showing an empty list, not an error. Live login to `/account` wasn't exercised (no known password for `arjayrafaelical@gmail.com`'s member session), but a direct DB check confirmed its underlying data is exactly as expected post-cleanup (0 bookings, 1 membership still attached) and `RecentBookingsList.tsx` already has a proper zero-length empty state ("No bookings yet."), so no live-render risk. One other non-member `Customer` row (`winstonsipandserve@gmail.com`, from earlier anonymous-booking tests) confirmed still present, along with all 6 pre-existing `Customer` rows.

- 2026-08-28 — Admin Dashboard wired to real Prisma queries, replacing the hardcoded mock data: `dashboard-mock-data.ts` deleted, new `src/lib/dashboard-data.ts` exports `getDashboardData()` (same `DashboardStats`/`BookingsTrendPoint`/`RevenueTrendPoint`/`ResourceBreakdownEntry`/`RecentBooking`/`RecentApplication` interfaces, unchanged). `page.tsx` is now an async server component fetching once and threading props down; the 3 chart/stat/activity components now take props instead of importing mock consts — no visual change. New PH-week/month window helpers (`currentPhWeekWindow`/`currentPhMonthWindow`/`phMonthStartUtc`/`toPhMonthKey`) added to `business-hours.ts` following its existing fixed-UTC+8-no-DST convention; `formatShortDate` added to `format.ts`. Resource Utilization % = booked hours (confirmed bookings, active resources, current PH week) ÷ (active resource count × business hours × 7 days). Reused the existing `formatTier()` switch (duplicated as a local, unexported helper in both admin memberships pages) rather than extracting a new shared module. Verified live: zero-state renders cleanly (no NaN/crash) against the real empty DB, then a temporary seed (`scripts/seed-dashboard-test-data.ts`, gitignored, `--cleanup` flag) exercised every stat/chart/list with a hand-computed expected result — all matched exactly (Bookings Today 2, Revenue This Month ₱2,000.00, Resource Utilization 1%, resource-type breakdown, Recent Bookings/Applications ordering) — then cleaned up back to the empty state. `/admin/bookings`/`/admin/memberships`/`/admin/resources`/`/admin/bulletin` confirmed unaffected. `tsc --noEmit` clean; zero brand-token matches in all touched files. Flagged, not fixed: neither Recent Bookings nor Pending Membership Applications has an explicit empty-state message — an empty list currently just renders no `<li>` rows.

- 2026-08-28 — Doc-only: the three byte-identical `formatTier` duplicates (both admin memberships pages + `dashboard-data.ts`, flagged but left in place by the prior Dashboard entry) extracted into a single shared `formatMembershipTier` export in `format.ts`, following the existing shared-helper convention (`booking-hold.ts`/`booking-pricing.ts`/`bulletin-validation.ts`). - 2026-08-28 — A 4th `formatMembershipTier` duplicate was found in `membership-pricing.ts` (used by `/account` and the admin membership-approval API route) during the earlier `format.ts` consolidation; confirmed byte-identical to `format.ts`'s version and consolidated in this pass — `membership-pricing.ts` now only exports `MEMBERSHIP_TIER_PLANS`/`computeMembershipEndDate`, all 5 call sites in the codebase now import `formatMembershipTier` from `format.ts`. - 2026-08-28 — Membership tier-activation payment (backend half) built: new `MembershipPayment` model (RLS deny-all, `prisma migrate deploy`) decouples payment from `Payment` (booking-scoped FK stays untouched — see new CLAUDE.md Architecture Decision). Admin approve (`PATCH /api/admin/memberships/[id]`) no longer creates a `Membership` — it only flips status + sends a new "complete your payment" email (`sendMembershipPaymentEmail`, 5th `buildBrandedEmail` consumer); `Membership`/credit-ledger/`MemberActivationToken` creation moved into a new additive `metadata.membershipPaymentId` branch on the PayMongo webhook, sitting alongside the untouched booking branch (dispatched by which metadata key is present; neither present is now a log/no-op, not a 400 — the old bookingId-required 400 no longer applies since the route now serves two payment kinds). New `POST /api/membership-payments` (create-or-resume checkout, mirrors `/api/checkout`'s resume pattern exactly, one `MembershipPayment` row per application unless still pending) and public `GET /api/membership-applications/[id]` (id/status/tierName/hasMembership only, no PII) routes. `sendActivationEmail` gained an optional receipt block (activation fee / F&B credit / total paid via the existing `ledgerRow` helper) — verified byte-identical HTML when the new params are omitted. `MembershipReviewActions.tsx`'s approve confirm-dialog copy updated to match the new flow. Verified live end-to-end: admin-UI approve (Playwright, `window.confirm` patched since the harness's browser can't answer native dialogs) → real Resend-delivered payment email → real `POST /api/membership-payments` PayMongo checkout (resume-on-repeat confirmed) → self-signed webhook → `Membership`/ledger/token/enhanced-activation-email all correct and matching `MEMBERSHIP_TIER_PLANS` exactly → replayed webhook confirmed idempotent (no duplicates) → reject branch and a full anonymous booking hold→checkout→webhook regression both confirmed unaffected. One gotcha hit and worked around, not a code defect: Resend rejects `@example.com` recipients outright (422), so verify customers needed real Gmail+alias addresses instead — same convention as prior verify passes. All test data (2 applications, 2 customers, 1 membership, ledger row, token, 1 booking+payment, 3 storage placeholder images) cleaned up via a scoped script, not the blanket `reset-membership-data.ts` (which would have also deleted the one real pre-existing `Membership` row) — confirmed post-cleanup counts exactly match pre-test baseline (1 membership, 6 customers, 1 application, 0 bookings). Also hit and resolved: local Prisma migration was blocked by the session-mode pooler's 15-connection cap (stale idle connections from a prior dev-server run) — Supabase MCP's role lacks `pg_signal_backend` so `pg_terminate_backend` 403'd; resolved instead by killing local `node.exe` processes (explicit user approval) — worth remembering as the actual fix now that the MCP-based one from 2026-08-27 is confirmed not to work with the current role. No customer-facing `/membership/pay/[id]` page exists yet — see Next Up.

New shared `membership-display-status.ts` backs a 4-state indicator (Pending/Approved—Awaiting Payment/Active Member/Rejected) on both admin Memberships pages, also fixing a pre-existing `bg-green-100` violation on the detail page found while touching it.

- 2026-08-28 — Doc-only reconciliation pass, discovered while verifying the membership-reminders work: CLAUDE.md's RLS bullet was stale at "17 tables" (real count, grep-verified: 19 — including `membership_payments`, added 2026-08-28, now the named example in place of the older `member_activation_tokens`) and its Transactional email branding bullet listed only 4 of the real 7 `buildBrandedEmail` consumers; this file's own Schema line model count was stale at "20" (real count: 19 — the prior breakdown miscounted the original base as 17 instead of 16, and had never accounted for `MembershipPayment`). No code touched.

- 2026-08-28 — Membership expiry/renewal reminders built end-to-end: 3 new nullable timestamp columns on `Membership` (`reminder14SentAt`/`reminder3SentAt`/`expiredNoticeSentAt`, hand-written migration `20260828030000_add_membership_reminder_tracking`), each backfilled from `end_date` against its own threshold so no pre-existing row fires an email it would have "missed" pre-feature (verified: the one real `Membership` row expires 2027-02-28, well outside all three windows, so all three columns correctly stayed `NULL` post-backfill). Two new `buildBrandedEmail` consumers in `resend.ts` — `sendMembershipExpiryReminderEmail` (14-day friendly / 3-day urgent tone, CTA to `/membership/apply`, credit-forfeiture line shown only when `creditBalanceCentavos > 0`) and `sendMembershipExpiredEmail` (calmer tone, mirrors `sendRejectionEmail`'s context-box pattern) — bringing the `buildBrandedEmail` consumer count to 7. New `GET /api/cron/membership-reminders` (mirrors `expire-bookings`'s `CRON_SECRET` bearer-auth pattern exactly), 3 sequential find-then-send-then-stamp passes (14-day / 3-day / expired), each row's sent-at column updated immediately after its send rather than batched, so a mid-run failure can't cause a retry to double-send; new daily `vercel.json` entry at `0 1 * * *` (staggered from `expire-bookings`'s midnight slot). Verified live end-to-end via a new scoped `scripts/verify-membership-reminders.ts`: 4 temporary Customer+MembershipApplication+Membership rows (10-days-out, 2-days-out, 2-days-ago, 20-days-out control) — real cron invocation confirmed exactly the 3 expected emails delivered (Resend logs) and the correct row-by-row sent-at columns, a second immediate invocation confirmed zero new sends (idempotency), and cleanup restored exact pre-test counts (6 customers / 1 application / 1 membership). One deliberate test-data design choice: the "2-days-out" row was seeded with `reminder14SentAt` pre-stamped (simulating an earlier cron cycle already having sent it), since a genuinely fresh row at that `endDate` would legitimately fire both the 14-day and 3-day reminders in the same run — the prompt's own spec calls this dual-fire correct, not a bug, but pre-stamping isolates the 3-day-only path more clearly for a one-shot verification. `tsc --noEmit` clean; RLS confirmed still enabled on `memberships` post-migration. Not re-run as full regression suites (spot-checked only, no code touched in these paths): `expire-bookings` cron, the other 5 `buildBrandedEmail` consumers, booking/membership-payment flows.

- 2026-08-28 — Closes out the 2026-08-28 "Doc-only reconciliation pass" entry above: CLAUDE.md's RLS table count and `buildBrandedEmail` consumer list are now actually corrected (19 tables / 7 consumers, both grep-verified against `schema.prisma` and `resend.ts`), and this file's own Schema line was corrected in the same pass.

- 2026-08-29 — Self-service membership renewal built end-to-end for already-activated members whose membership has expired. Schema: `Membership.applicationId` and `MembershipPayment.applicationId` are now optional (hand-written migration `20260829010000_add_membership_renewal_support`, `prisma migrate deploy`), and `MembershipPayment` gained a direct required `customerId` (backfilled from the existing `application_id` join), since a renewal has no `MembershipApplication` to reach the customer through; `CreditTransactionReason` gained a new `renewal` value alongside the existing `activation`. The PayMongo webhook's `handleMembershipPaymentWebhook` now branches on `membershipPayment.applicationId === null`: a renewal creates a brand-new `Membership` row with `applicationId: null`, a `reason: 'renewal'` ledger row, and sends the new `sendMembershipRenewalEmail` (8th `buildBrandedEmail` consumer, reuses `sendActivationEmail`'s receipt-block pattern) instead of ever touching `MemberActivationToken`/`sendActivationEmail` — the pre-existing first-time application→approval→payment path is unchanged, just reading `membershipPayment.customer`/`.tier` instead of `application.customer`/`.requestedTier` now that those are directly on `MembershipPayment`. New session-gated `POST /api/account/membership-renewal` (401 if not a member session, 409 if already active, create-or-resume checkout mirroring `POST /api/membership-payments`' pattern) and public `GET /api/membership-payments/[id]` (id/status/tierName/hasMembership only, no PII, mirrors `GET /api/membership-applications/[id]`) back two new customer-facing pages: `/account/renew` (3 tier cards, redirects to `/account` if already active or `/membership/apply` if the customer has no membership history at all) and `/account/renew/confirmation` (polling, mirrors `/membership/pay/confirmation` minus the rejected-state branch). `MembershipStatusCard` now shows a "Renew Membership" CTA above "Book a Court" only when `isExpired` is true; "Book a Court" itself is unchanged and always renders. No admin-side "Send Renewal Link" action built — flagged in Next Up as a separate follow-up.

- 2026-08-29 — Doc-only: reconciled three CLAUDE.md bullets left stale by the 2026-08-29 renewal feature (Membership payment model, Membership credit ledger, Transactional email branding consumer count 7→8) — that prompt only updated PROGRESS.md at the time.

- 2026-08-29 — Fixed two real bugs in the admin Memberships display-status badge, both stemming from `getMembershipDisplayStatus` deriving state from `application.membership` (the application's own dangling 1:1 link) instead of the customer's actual current membership: (1) an "Active Member" badge never actually checked `endDate`, so an expired-but-never-renewed membership still showed active; (2) a renewed customer's newer `applicationId: null` Membership row was invisible to this lookup entirely, since it isn't linked to the original application. `getMembershipDisplayStatus` now takes `latestMembership: { endDate } | null` (the customer's most recent `Membership` row of any status/`applicationId`, same resolution `/account` already used for a single customer) and derives a new `expired` state via a real `endDate >= now` check. New `src/lib/membership-latest.ts` (`getLatestMembershipsByCustomerIds` batched + `getLatestMembershipByCustomerId` wrapper) is shared by the list page (one batched query, not N+1) and the detail page, so the two can never drift. `pending`/`rejected`/`awaiting_payment` states are unchanged.

- 2026-08-29 — Admin-side "Send Renewal Link" action built, closing out the manual fallback flagged when self-service renewal shipped. Schema: `MembershipPayment.initiatedByAdminId` (nullable FK to `AdminUser`, hand-written migration `20260829020000_add_membership_payment_initiated_by_admin`, RLS/deny-all reconfirmed post-migration) tracks which admin sent a link, mirroring the `reviewedBy`/`performedBy` accountability pattern. New `sendRenewalPaymentLinkEmail` (9th `buildBrandedEmail` consumer). New session-gated `POST /api/admin/memberships/[id]/send-renewal-link` (admin-only, reuses `getLatestMembershipByCustomerId` for the same expired-only guard the button's own visibility already enforces client-side, create-or-resume against a pending renewal `MembershipPayment` so repeat sends never duplicate a row) and public `POST /api/membership-payments/[id]/checkout` (mirrors `POST /api/membership-payments`'s checkout-session creation block, 409s if already paid or if `applicationId` isn't null). New customer-facing `/membership/renew/[id]` (mirrors `/membership/pay/[id]`) + `CompleteRenewalPaymentButton`, and admin-facing `SendRenewalLinkButton` (neutral-gray tier-picker modal, reuses the shared `Modal` component the same way `MembershipReviewActions`' `RejectModal` already does) rendered on `/admin/memberships/[id]` only when `displayStatus === 'expired'`. Zero webhook changes needed — the existing `applicationId === null` dispatch branch already treats an admin-initiated renewal payment identically to a self-service one. Confirmation-page reuse (`/account/renew/confirmation`) verified session-free, as required for an admin-sent link a customer clicks with no active session.

- 2026-08-29 — First real Vercel build attempt (on `dev`, carrying the admin renewal-link work) failed at `tsc` with dozens of "property does not exist" errors spanning nearly every model added since the original schema (`passwordHash`, `MemberActivationToken`, `MembershipPayment`, `Bulletin.imageUrl`/`socialPlatform`, etc.). Root cause was a stale cached Prisma Client, not any application bug: Vercel's `node_modules` cache persists across deploys when the lockfile is unchanged, so `@prisma/client`'s postinstall-only generation was silently never re-running. Fixed by changing `package.json`'s `build` script to `"prisma generate && next build"`, making generation unskippable on every build regardless of install-cache state. This is a build-infra issue, unrelated to the renewal feature itself, which was otherwise unaffected (no logic bug in any of the 3 renewal prompts).

- 2026-08-29 — Diagnosed and fixed two stacked Vercel deploy blockers found while redeploying `dev`/`staging` after the Prisma-cache fix: (1) Vercel Authentication ("Standard Protection") was gating every deployment project-wide, including Production, since no custom domain exists yet to exempt it — turned off to unblock testing/review/webhooks; (2) the actual root cause of every 404 regardless of build success — Project Settings' Framework Preset was set to "Other" instead of "Next.js", likely since project creation — meant Vercel never wired up Next.js's routing/serverless functions even on a clean build. Fixed Framework Preset to Next.js and redeployed `dev`/`staging`; both confirmed Ready and rendering the real site live via direct URL check. Production still runs on its own pinned "Production Overrides" tied to the original Aug 7 stub — will only pick up the fix once actually promoted (staging → main).

Production's `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` updated from the placeholder `winston-booking-site.vercel.app` to `https://winstonsipandserve.club` in the same pass. - 2026-08-29 — Investigated the `bookings_customer_id_fkey` mismatch flagged in Next Up. Found `ON UPDATE CASCADE` already matched the live constraint; the real gap was `schema.prisma` never explicitly annotating `onDelete` on `Booking.customer`, defaulting to Prisma's implicit `SetNull` for an optional relation, against a live constraint that is actually `RESTRICT`. Decided RESTRICT is correct — it matches the app's existing "disable, don't delete" precedent (`Resource.isActive`) and no live delete-customer feature exists — and made `schema.prisma` say so explicitly (`onDelete: Restrict, onUpdate: Cascade`). No migration needed; the database was already correct, and the live constraint was re-queried afterward and confirmed byte-identical to before this change.

All/Pending/Rejected keep the original DB-paginated query untouched; the 3 derived sub-filters fetch every `approved` application once (`relationLoadStrategy: 'join'`, matching this page's pre-existing exception), derive each row's display status via the existing `getMembershipDisplayStatus`, filter in memory, then paginate the filtered array in JS. Verified live via Playwright against a temporary seeded admin + 37 temporary applications spanning all 6 states (including 27 `active` rows to cross a page boundary within a derived filter), all deleted after the run: each tab shows the exact expected row count and paginates correctly (Active: 25 on page 1, 3 on page 2 — the extra row being one genuinely-active pre-existing membership, not a bug); `/admin/memberships/[id]` opens correctly for a representative row in all 6 states; `/admin`, `/admin/bookings`, `/admin/resources`, `/admin/bulletin` regression-clean. `tsc --noEmit` clean; only `src/app/admin/(protected)/memberships/page.tsx` touched.

- 2026-08-29 — Performance fix for `/admin/memberships`: Vercel runtime logs had shown `memberships:pageTotal` at 2.422s against a ~200ms actual Prisma commit, traced to the 2026-08-29 display-status fix's `getLatestMembershipsByCustomerIds` call paying a second full connection-priming round trip as a separate query after the main list fetch. Folded into the same query via a nested `customer: { include: { memberships: { orderBy: { startDate: 'desc' }, take: 1 } } }` include (confirmed via Prisma query-event logging to compile to a single query with a `LEFT JOIN LATERAL` under the existing `relationLoadStrategy: 'join'`, not N+1), removing one top-level Prisma call per branch; `latestMembershipsByCustomer` is now derived from the already-fetched nested data instead of a second round trip. `membership-latest.ts` (still the source of truth for the `[id]` detail page), `membership-display-status.ts`, and the detail page itself are untouched (byte-identical, confirmed via `git diff`). Local `memberships:pageTotal` measured 595.66ms post-fix (was not independently re-measured pre-fix locally, since the 2.422s baseline was a Vercel-runtime number, not local — local dev's session-mode pooler doesn't exhibit the same connection-priming cost pattern as staging/production's transaction-mode pooler per the existing CLAUDE.md note); the query-count reduction itself (2 top-level queries instead of 3, confirmed via live query logs) is the change that removes the extra priming round trip wherever it occurs. `tsc --noEmit` clean. Verified live via the Browser pane against a temporary 37-row seed (`scripts/seed-membership-status-filters.ts`, reused as-is) spanning all 6 derived statuses: every filter tab (All/Pending/Awaiting Payment/Active/Expired/Rejected) and pagination boundary (Active: 25 rows page 1, 3 page 2) rendered identical content to before; all 5 non-pending detail-page statuses spot-checked; `/admin`, `/admin/bookings`, `/admin/resources`, `/admin/bulletin` regression-clean. Cleanup confirmed via `scripts/seed-membership-status-filters.ts --cleanup` — post-cleanup counts (6 customers / 1 application / 1 membership) match the pre-seed baseline exactly.

- 2026-08-29 — Diagnosed a Vercel/Supabase region mismatch as the dominant cause of the noticeable `/admin/*` latency on deployed `dev`: `x-vercel-id` response headers showed serverless functions executing in `iad1` (US East/Virginia) — Vercel's default, since `vercel.json` had no `regions` field — while the Supabase project runs in `ap-southeast-2` (Sydney). Every DB round trip was crossing that distance. Supavisor pooler logs corroborated it: each navigation produced a fresh burst of `Backend authenticated` events followed by the connection closing within seconds, confirming per-invocation connection setup with no reuse across requests, on top of the transcontinental RTT. Measured live (logged in, real clicks, no code changed): login `POST /admin/login` ~5,300ms; Bookings/Resources & Pricing/Memberships/Bulletin tab navigations ~1,600–1,900ms each. Read-only investigation — no fix applied in this pass (Vercel MCP access didn't extend to this project's server-side runtime logs, so evidence came from response headers, client-side Performance-API timing, and Supabase's own logs).

- 2026-08-30 — Re-verified after the region fix: Vercel Function Region changed to `syd1` (Sydney) to match Supabase's `ap-southeast-2`, `dev` redeployed. `x-vercel-id` now reads `sin1::syd1::...` on both page and API routes (was `sin1::iad1::...`). Re-measured the same actions logged in with real clicks: login `POST /admin/login` dropped from ~5,300ms to 822ms; Dashboard 318ms; Bookings dropped from ~1,900ms to 330ms; Resources & Pricing from ~1,895ms to 309ms; Memberships from ~1,860ms to 311ms; Bulletin from ~1,618ms to 294ms — a consistent ~82–85% reduction across every action, including login, confirming the region mismatch was the dominant cause rather than one of several contributing factors. The ~300ms now remaining per tab is in line with the separately-tracked connection-priming floor (see Next Up) — a smaller, already-known cost, not evidence the region fix under-delivered. Doc-only/read-only pass — no code, env vars, or further Vercel settings changed.

- 2026-08-30 — Reconciled the `/api/resources` round-trip discrepancy flagged in Next Up: measured directly against the deployed `dev` URL via real `fetch()` calls and cold full-page navigations to `/book` (where this endpoint fires on load). Results: 175–259ms across 5 warm fetches, 222ms and 687ms on two separate cold navigations — `x-vercel-id` confirmed `sin1::syd1` on every call, i.e. already running in the post-region-fix region. Neither stale figure holds up: the real cost lines up with the already-documented ~300ms connection-priming floor (occasionally higher on a genuinely cold invocation), not the old 1.3–4s or 20–100ms numbers. Most likely explanation: the 1.3–4s instrumentation reading predated the 2026-08-30 region fix (same `iad1`-vs-Sydney cause that hit every admin route), and the 20–100ms reading was a Browser-pane measurement artifact, the same failure mode confirmed separately on the AdminSidebar fold-button investigation. - 2026-08-30 — Closed out the connection-priming-floor investigation flagged in Next Up: confirmed via the Vercel dashboard (Project Settings → Functions) that Fluid Compute is already **Enabled** on this project, and Function Region is set to Asia Pacific (`syd1`), matching the earlier region fix — both were default/already-applied, not missing. Fluid Compute has been Vercel's default for all new projects since April 2025, and this project was scaffolded after that date, so the ~300ms floor already reflects Fluid Compute's warm-instance connection reuse, not an unoptimized baseline. Live measurement (see the `/api/resources` reconciliation entry above) already showed the expected warm/cold pattern — 175–260ms on warm hits, up to ~700ms on a cold instance — consistent with this. Two further levers were researched and deliberately deferred, not pursued: switching from Supabase's PgBouncer transaction-mode pooler to a native `pg` Pool + Vercel's `attachDatabasePool` (free, but a real `src/lib/prisma.ts` refactor plus a new dependency for a further ~100–200ms on an internal-only admin panel), and Prisma Accelerate (low code change, but a recurring paid service plus a new dependency for the same marginal gain). Decision: not worth the added complexity/cost right now given this is admin-panel-only latency, not customer-facing checkout latency — revisit only if `/admin/*` traffic or scope grows enough to matter. No code changed.

- 2026-08-31 — Fixed all 5 re-verified Home mobile findings (`TwoSides.tsx`, `Navbar.tsx`, `StatsBar.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`): **(1)** TwoSides' "Book a Court →" link now sits on an opaque mobile-only backdrop (`bg-brand-light`, `rounded-none`, `px-3 py-2`, reverting to `md:bg-transparent md:px-0 md:py-0`) — deviated from the prompt's suggested `bg-brand-dark/70` starting point because a dark backdrop caps out at **2.79:1 even at full opacity** (computed: `text-accent-primary` #CD1818 has too low a luminance itself to clear 4.5:1 against any dark backdrop, no matter the opacity), so a light backdrop was used instead — measured **5.12:1 at all 4 widths** (320/375/414/639), position-independent since the backdrop is fully opaque; New `GET /api/admin/check-in/[token]/route.ts` (admin-session-gated, mirrors `memberships/[id]/route.ts`'s 401 check exactly): 404 `{ error: 'Code not recognized' }` for an unrecognized token, 200 `{ found: true, hasMembership: false, name }` for a customer with no membership, 200 with full tier/expiry/credit fields (via `getLatestMembershipByCustomerId` + `buildMembershipDisplayFields`) for one with a membership — no email/PII included on the no-membership or not-found branches. New `src/app/admin/(protected)/check-in/page.tsx` (server component, mirrors the bulletin page's heading/card shell) renders it. `AdminSidebar.tsx` gained a 6th "Check-In" nav entry (appended at the end) and `AdminIcons.tsx` gained a new hand-authored `CheckInIcon` (scan-frame-with-inner-square, matching the existing 1.5 stroke-width/24×24 viewBox convention). Verified live end-to-end against temporary seed data (new gitignored `scripts/verify-admin-check-in-scan.ts`, mirroring `verify-admin-renewal-link.ts`'s real-HTTP-login convention via `/api/auth/csrf` + `/api/auth/callback/credentials`, removed after): direct authenticated `GET` calls confirmed all 4 cases (active → 200 with correct tier/expiry/credit fields, expired → 200 `isExpired: true`, no-membership → 200 `hasMembership: false` with no email key, garbage token → 404) plus a 401 for an unauthenticated call; `/admin/check-in` loaded live as an authenticated admin, rendering the new sidebar tab (correctly highlighted active) and the graceful camera-denied fallback message (no crash, no uncaught console errors — only the deliberate `console.error` diagnostic log in the catch branch); all other 5 admin tabs (Dashboard, Bookings, Resources & Pricing, Memberships, Bulletin) regression-spot-checked unaffected; `/account` re-verified live for the seeded active member post-refactor, rendering values byte-identical to the API response (3-Month, ₱2,000.00 activation + ₱3,500.00 F&B credit, ₱3,500.00 of ₱3,500.00 remaining, September 30, 2026 expiry) — the parallel expired-member browser login attempt was flaky in this automation environment (session didn't switch after a second credentials submit) and wasn't forced further, since `/account` and the verify endpoint call the exact same `buildMembershipDisplayFields` function, and the expired case was already proven correct via the direct authenticated API call. `tsc --noEmit` clean. **Known limitation, not fully verified**: a real end-to-end camera scan of a physical/on-screen QR code cannot be exercised by browser automation (no real camera feed available) — Arjay should manually verify this final step with a real phone/laptop camera against a real member's `/account` QR code.
