- 2026-08-31 — F&B membership credit now has a real spend path: a member booking through the session-aware direct-booking route (`POST /api/bookings`) is confirmed instantly and paid via `Membership.creditBalanceCentavos` when that balance fully covers the grand total — full-or-nothing by design, no partial-credit application. Schema additions (hand-written migration `20260831020000_add_membership_credit_redemption_support`, RLS reconfirmed intact post-migration on `payments`/`membership_credit_transactions`/`memberships`/`bookings`): `CreditTransactionReason` gained `booking_redemption`; `MembershipCreditTransaction` gained a nullable `bookingId` FK (null for pre-existing activation/renewal rows); `Payment` gained a `method` enum (`paymongo` default / `membership_credit`) so a credit-covered booking's `Payment` row is distinguishable from a real PayMongo charge. The credit check/decrement happens inside the same `$transaction` as booking creation, guarded by an `updateMany({ where: { creditBalanceCentavos: { gte: grandTotal } } })` rather than a read-then-write — this is what makes two concurrent bookings racing the same balance resolve to exactly one `creditCovered: true`, never a double-spend or a negative balance. `getActiveMembership` was extracted alongside the existing `isActiveMember` in `customer-resolution.ts` (byte-identical behavior for existing callers) so the route has the real `Membership` row, not just a boolean, to check the balance against and decrement. Booking-confirmation-email logic (previously duplicated inline in the PayMongo webhook handler) was extracted into a new shared `src/lib/booking-confirmation.ts` (`sendBookingConfirmationEmailForBooking`, optional `creditRedemption` param) so both the webhook's `payment.paid` path and the new instant-confirm path send the identical email shape; `resend.ts`'s template gained an optional "This booking was covered by your F&amp;B credit. You have X remaining." notice block, rendered only when that param is passed. `BookingForm.tsx` redirects straight to `/book/confirmation` on `creditCovered: true`, skipping the Payment step and `/api/checkout` entirely. Explicitly unchanged, per scope: `/api/checkout`, the anonymous hold-then-`PATCH`-attach path, `PaymentStep.tsx`, `booking-pricing.ts`'s pricing math, and anything membership-application/renewal-related — a member with insufficient credit (or no membership, or an anonymous booker) goes through the exact pre-existing PayMongo flow with no behavior change. Verified end-to-end via a temporary seeded set (admin + 4 members: full-cover, insufficient, lapsed, concurrent-race; new gitignored `scripts/verify-membership-credit-redemption.ts`, removed after): full-cover case produced a `confirmed` booking, a `membership_credit`/`paid` `Payment` with both PayMongo id fields null, a `-grandTotal` `booking_redemption` ledger row, and a `Membership` balance decremented to exactly 0 — confirmed both via direct DB checks and a real Resend-delivered confirmation email showing the credit note; insufficient-credit case confirmed the pre-existing flow completely unchanged, including a self-signed `payment.paid` webhook simulation (matching the real PayMongo payload shape documented elsewhere in this file) correctly confirming the booking with `method` staying `paymongo` and no credit note in that email; anonymous and lapsed-membership bookings regression-clean (`isMember: false`, non-member pricing, unaffected); concurrency case fired two real concurrent `POST /api/bookings` calls against a balance sized for exactly one — exactly one came back `creditCovered: true`, the other a normal `pending_payment` booking, final balance exactly 0 (not negative, not double-spent); admin `/admin/bookings` list + detail page both confirmed rendering the credit-covered booking cleanly with no code changes needed (grep confirmed neither page has any UI assumption tied to `paymongoCheckoutSessionId`/`paymongoPaymentIntentId` existing). Also confirmed live via the Browser pane: submitting the real booking wizard as a fully-covered member redirects straight to `/book/confirmation` with zero `/api/checkout` network calls made. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `git status` confirmed no out-of-scope files touched. Post-cleanup counts (8 customers / 1 membership / 3 bookings / 2 payments / 1 credit transaction / 2 admin users) match the pre-test baseline exactly.

- 2026-08-31 — Three member sign-in/sign-out fixes on the public site. `LoginForm.tsx` converted from a server action (`signIn(...redirectTo)`) to client-side `signIn('member-credentials', { redirect: false })` + `router.push('/account')` — fixes the Navbar not reflecting a fresh login until a tab switch/refocus, since the server-action path never touched `next-auth/react`'s client session context that `useSession()` reads. `/login` no longer has a `?error=1` query param; the "Invalid email or password." message is now local component state. Added a password show/hide toggle to `/login` via the existing `PasswordInput` component (brand-toned overrides, not its default admin gray styling). `Navbar.tsx`'s Sign Out (desktop and mobile) now opens a confirmation `Modal` before calling `signOut()`, mirroring `MembershipStatusCard`'s public/brand-token confirm-modal pattern (`rounded-none` buttons) rather than the admin `SignOutButton`'s gray styling. Admin login/sign-out (separate files) untouched. Verified live via the Browser pane against a temporary seeded member (new gitignored `scripts/verify-login-flow.ts`, removed after): password toggle shows/hides/reverts correctly with value preserved and no accidental submit; correct login updates the Navbar to the signed-in state immediately (no tab switch) and lands on `/account`; wrong credentials show the inline error with fields preserved and a clean URL; Sign Out confirmation modal (desktop and mobile, mobile menu closes cleanly first) both Cancels (stays signed in) and Confirms (signs out, Navbar reverts immediately); Escape and backdrop-click both dismiss the modal without signing out. `/admin/login` regression-spot-checked unaffected. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `git status` confirmed only `LoginForm.tsx`, `login/page.tsx`, and `Navbar.tsx` changed. Post-cleanup customer count matches the pre-test baseline exactly.

- 2026-08-31 — `priority`/`BulletinPriority` removed from `Bulletin` entirely (schema column + enum, validation, both admin API routes, the admin Add/Edit form's Priority section, `/news`, `AnnouncementGate`, `NewsCard`'s two High-Priority badge blocks, and the seed script) — client/business decision that announcements shouldn't carry a priority. Hand-written migration `20260831030000_drop_bulletin_priority` (drops the column then the enum type); RLS reconfirmed intact on `bulletins` post-migration. `bulletinOrderBy` simplified to `publishedAt: 'desc'` only. `tsc --noEmit` clean; re-ran `scripts/seed-sample-bulletins.ts` (6 rows updated, no column error); browser-verified live as admin — the Add/Edit modal has no Priority field, a new bulletin saves successfully, and editing one of the 6 pre-existing rows (which had a stored `priority` value before the drop) saves with no error; `/news` and `/book`'s `AnnouncementGate` both render all bulletins with no High Priority badge; other 4 admin tabs regression-spot-checked unaffected. Found in passing, not fixed here (out of scope): `scripts/` is entirely gitignored (`.gitignore` line 36), so `seed-sample-bulletins.ts` is not actually git-tracked despite its own header comment describing it as persistent/reusable — flagging since a future clean checkout won't have it.

- 2026-08-31 — Fixed a "Cannot pause, scanner is not scanning" unhandled rejection in `CheckInScanner.tsx`, caused by `handleDecode` having no guard against being invoked more than once per scan cycle before `pause()` took effect. Added a synchronous `isHandlingScanRef` guard (set on first decode, before any async work; cleared in `handleScanNext`) so duplicate decode events are dropped instead of calling `pause()` again. Client-only fix, no schema/API changes, no seed/cleanup needed.

- 2026-08-31 — Added a 6-digit fallback check-in code alongside the existing QR token, for admin manual entry when scanning isn't practical. New `Customer.checkInCode` column (nullable, unique, `VARCHAR(6)` zero-padded, hand-written migration `20260831040000_add_customer_check_in_code`, RLS reconfirmed intact post-migration). `check-in-token.ts`'s `getOrCreateCheckInToken`/`regenerateCheckInToken` now always create/rotate the token and code together in one `update()` call (never independently) and return `{ token, code }`; a new collision-retry pattern (`isUniqueCheckInCodeViolation` catching Prisma `P2002` on the `check_in_code` constraint, up to 10 attempts) was introduced here since nothing existing needed it. Admin lookup logic was extracted into a new shared `src/lib/admin-check-in.ts` (`resolveAdminCheckInResult`) so the pre-existing `GET /api/admin/check-in/[token]` and a new `GET /api/admin/check-in/code/[code]` route produce byte-identical response shapes — confirmed via a live side-by-side call in the verify script. `/admin/check-in` gained a Scan/Enter Code tab toggle (`CheckInTabs.tsx`); the scan result card was extracted into `CheckInResultCard.tsx` so both paths render identically, and a new `CheckInCodeEntry.tsx` rejects anything other than exactly 6 digits client-side before any network call. `/account`'s `MembershipStatusCard` now displays "Check-in code: NNNNNN" under the QR thumbnail and in the full-screen modal, updating in place (no reload) when Regenerate is confirmed. Verified live end-to-end (new gitignored `scripts/verify-check-in-code-fallback.ts`, mirroring `verify-admin-check-in-scan.ts`'s convention): active/expired/no-membership/garbage codes all correct, token-route regression byte-identical, two consecutive regenerates confirmed no stale-code carryover, and the admin UI tab toggle/code entry/result cards all confirmed live via the Browser pane (including client-side digit-only filtering and the sub-6-digit block). `tsc --noEmit` clean; `git status` confirmed scope matched exactly (check-in files only); post-cleanup counts (8 customers / 1 membership / 2 admin users) match the pre-test baseline exactly. Deferred, not forgotten: no rate-limiting on code-entry attempts — a 6-digit code has only 1,000,000 possibilities, so this is worth revisiting before this becomes a public-facing (rather than staff-operated, behind admin auth) surface.

- 2026-08-31 — Fixed a documentation gap in CLAUDE.md — the member check-in (QR + fallback code) decision had only ever lived as a stale "Open / Not Yet Decided" bullet (incorrectly implying admin-side scan/verify wasn't built, and not reflecting the 2026-08-31 code-fallback addition at all). Moved the full, current decision into Architecture Decisions; the Open/Not Yet Decided entry is now a short pointer to it, avoiding duplicated/stale detail. Docs-only, no code changed.

- 2026-08-31 — Added `sendStaffBookingNotificationEmail`, `buildBrandedEmail`'s 10th consumer — an internal notification to `winstonsipandserve@gmail.com` (the existing `REPLY_TO_ADDRESS`) firing alongside the customer confirmation on every booking that transitions to `confirmed`, wired into the single shared `sendBookingConfirmationEmailForBooking` call site so both the PayMongo-webhook-paid and credit-redeemed instant-confirm paths cover it automatically with no risk of drift. Reuses the exact same ledger-row/ledger-data computation as the customer email (guest fee, add-ons, total) plus a customer contact block (name/email/phone) and a payment-method line ("Paid via PayMongo" or "Paid via F&B Credit — X applied, Y remaining"), and a "View in Admin" CTA linking to `/admin/bookings/{id}` (still gated by the existing admin auth check — no new unauthenticated surface). Subject line establishes a `[Booking] New booking — {resourceLabel} — {date}, {time}` convention, intended to be reused by future staff-notification email types (membership application/approval/payment/renewal — see Next Up). Purely additive — no schema change, no new env var, `sendBookingConfirmationEmail`'s own body untouched (confirmed via `git diff` showing zero removed lines in `resend.ts`). Verified end-to-end via a temporary seeded pair (new gitignored `scripts/verify-staff-booking-notification.ts`): a webhook-paid anonymous tennis booking (guestCount 1 + Ball Boy add-on) and a credit-redeemed member pickleball booking (no guest fee, no add-ons) both confirmed via direct DB checks, then both staff + both customer emails confirmed actually delivered and pulled in full via the Resend MCP — staff email ledger rows matched the customer email's exactly (guest fee row present/absent, add-on row present/absent, in the same order), payment-method line correct for each case, and both "View in Admin" links resolved to the correct booking id. `booking_no_overlap` exclusion constraint reconfirmed intact via `pg_constraint` (unrelated to this change, per standing convention since the code touches `Booking` rows). `tsc --noEmit` clean; `git status` confirmed only `src/lib/resend.ts`, `src/lib/booking-confirmation.ts`, and `PROGRESS.md` changed. Post-cleanup counts (8 customers / 5 bookings / 4 payments) match the pre-test baseline exactly.

- 2026-09-01 — Added `sendStaffMembershipApplicationEmail`, `buildBrandedEmail`'s 11th consumer, wired directly into `POST /api/membership-applications` right after `prisma.membershipApplication.create` succeeds — continues the `[Booking]`-style convention from the 2026-08-31 staff booking notification with its own `[Membership Application]` subject prefix. Sends to the existing `REPLY_TO_ADDRESS` staff inbox with the applicant's name/email/contact number/address, requested tier (via the already-imported `formatMembershipTier`), submitted date, and a "View in Admin" CTA to `/admin/memberships/{applicationId}` — deliberately excludes the gov-ID image URLs, since that storage bucket is private and "View in Admin" is the intended way to view them. Purely additive: no schema change, no new env var, fires only after a successful create (validation failures, the 409 duplicate-pending guard, and upload failures never reach it), and doesn't touch the existing `uploadedPaths` storage-cleanup path. Verified end-to-end via a temporary seeded set (new gitignored `scripts/verify-staff-membership-application-notification.ts`): all three tiers submitted successfully with the staff email firing each time (confirmed via the Resend MCP — correct subject/tier/contact details/View-in-Admin link, no gov-ID URLs anywhere in the HTML body); a missing-field submission correctly 400'd with no `Customer` row created and no email; a duplicate-pending submission correctly 409'd with no duplicate application row and no email; a repeat applicant (existing `Customer` resolved via `resolveCustomer`, updated name/phone) correctly triggered a fresh staff email reflecting the updated contact details, with no duplicate `Customer` row. Regression-confirmed the existing admin approve/reject flow (`sendMembershipPaymentEmail`/`sendRejectionEmail`) and the 2026-08-31 `sendStaffBookingNotificationEmail` path both still fire unaffected. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `git status` confirmed only `src/lib/resend.ts`, `src/app/api/membership-applications/route.ts`, and `PROGRESS.md` changed. Post-cleanup counts (8 customers / 1 membership application) match the pre-test baseline exactly.

- 2026-09-01 — Added `sendStaffMembershipActivationEmail` (`[Membership Payment]`) and `sendStaffMembershipRenewalEmail` (`[Membership Renewal]`), `buildBrandedEmail`'s 12th and 13th consumers, wired into `handleMembershipPaymentWebhook` in `src/app/api/webhooks/paymongo/route.ts` — closes out the last two staff-notification types alongside booking and application submission. As a behavior-preserving refactor, `tierName` (`formatMembershipTier(membershipPayment.tier)`) and `expiryDateLabel` (the `Intl.DateTimeFormat` computation previously local to the renewal branch) were hoisted to right after `endDate` is computed, so both new functions and the pre-existing `sendMembershipRenewalEmail`/`sendActivationEmail` calls all read the same values — confirmed byte-identical customer-email output since neither existing function's body changed at all (`git diff` on `resend.ts` shows a pure insertion). The new staff activation email fires unconditionally on every non-renewal membership payment, deliberately not gated behind the `!membershipPayment.customer.passwordHash` check that gates `sendActivationEmail`/token creation — this is what lets it also cover the edge case of a payment for a customer who already has a password (e.g. re-approved after a prior membership), where the customer-facing activation email correctly stays silent. The renewal staff email has no CTA/link at all (mirrors `sendRejectionEmail`'s no-CTA pattern) since `Membership.applicationId` is null by design for renewals, so there's no reliable admin record to link to. Verified end-to-end via a temporary seeded set (new gitignored `scripts/verify-staff-membership-payment-notifications.ts`, removed after): first-time activation (no `passwordHash`) fired both the customer activation email and the new staff email with correct ledger/tier/expiry and a working "View Application" link; the no-token edge case (customer already has a `passwordHash`) confirmed the customer email correctly did NOT fire while the staff email still did, proving the two sends are decoupled; renewal fired both the customer renewal email and the new staff email, the latter's HTML confirmed via the Resend MCP to contain no CTA table/link anywhere; a redelivered webhook (same `membershipPaymentId`, already `paid`) confirmed the existing early-return still prevents all four emails from double-firing (no duplicate `Membership` rows created). Regression-confirmed via the same script: a real booking through to `confirmed` still fires `sendStaffBookingNotificationEmail`, and a membership application submission still fires `sendStaffMembershipApplicationEmail`. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `git status` confirmed only `src/lib/resend.ts`, `src/app/api/webhooks/paymongo/route.ts`, and `PROGRESS.md` changed. Post-cleanup counts (8 customers / 1 membership / 0 pending membership payments / 3 membership credit transactions / 6 bookings) match the pre-test baseline exactly.

- 2026-09-01 — Added an insufficient-credit advisory modal at the booking Review step for active members whose F&B credit balance won't fully cover the booking total (including exactly zero), gated behind a new `handleConfirmBookingClick` check in `BookingForm.tsx` before `POST /api/bookings` ever fires. `memberContext` now carries `creditBalanceCentavos` via `getActiveMembership` (replacing the boolean-only `isActiveMember` call in `book/page.tsx`) — same single query as before, just exposing the full row. UI-only/advisory: the server's atomic full-or-nothing check in `POST /api/bookings` remains the sole enforcement point, completely unchanged. Verified end-to-end via a temporary seeded set (gitignored `scripts/verify-insufficient-credit-modal.ts`, removed after): zero-balance member (correct zero-balance wording), partial-balance member (₱450 credit vs ₱650 total — correct amounts shown, "Continue Booking" proceeds through pending_payment → checkout → webhook-paid, credit balance confirmed untouched at both checkpoints via direct DB query), full-cover member (no modal, instant credit-covered redirect regression-confirmed live), and anonymous booking (no modal, unchanged flow regression-confirmed live) — all via the Browser pane. Escape key and backdrop click both dismiss the modal identically to "Go Back," with no request fired and all selections intact; "Start Over" leaves no stale modal state. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `git status` confirmed only `src/app/book/page.tsx`, `src/components/booking/BookingPageClient.tsx`, and `src/components/booking/BookingForm.tsx` changed.

- 2026-09-01 — Added rate limiting to `/admin/check-in`'s Enter Code lookup: a new `CheckInLookupAttempt` model (FK'd to `AdminUser.id`, `ON DELETE CASCADE`, isolated migration + RLS deny-all in `prisma/manual-sql/check-in-lookup-attempt-table.sql`) logs each failed code lookup, and `src/lib/check-in-rate-limit.ts` blocks the 11th failed lookup within any rolling 5-minute window per admin — a soft abuse-detection counter (DB-backed, expire-on-write cleanup, no locking/transaction needed), not a financial commitment. `GET /api/admin/check-in/code/[code]` now checks the limit immediately after the existing admin-session check and returns 429 (with a best-effort `retryAfterSeconds`) before ever querying `Customer` on a blocked attempt; successful lookups are never recorded. `CheckInCodeEntry.tsx` renders the 429 case through the same `CheckInResultCard` styling already used for "Code not recognized," with a distinct message. The QR/token route (`GET /api/admin/check-in/[token]`) is untouched and intentionally excluded — its 24-random-byte token space isn't brute-forceable. Verified end-to-end via a temporary seeded pair of admins (gitignored `scripts/verify-check-in-rate-limit.ts`, removed after): 10 failed lookups as Admin A recorded 10 rows and resolved normally, the 11th returned 429 (with `retryAfterSeconds`) without adding an 11th row and without resolving even a real member's valid code; Admin B's own lookups were unaffected and logged nothing on success; backdating Admin A's rows outside the 5-minute window (simulating expiry) correctly purged them via the opportunistic cleanup and unblocked the next lookup; 15 rapid token-route lookups were never blocked. Also confirmed live in the Browser pane as a real logged-in admin — the Enter Code tab correctly showed "Code not recognized" for a bad code and "Too many attempts. Try again in about 1 minute." after tripping the limit, both via the same red-bordered result card. `booking_no_overlap` exclusion constraint and the new table's RLS deny-all policies (`anon`+`authenticated`) reconfirmed via direct Postgres queries. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `git status` confirmed only `prisma/schema.prisma`, the new migration, `src/app/api/admin/check-in/code/[code]/route.ts`, `src/lib/check-in-rate-limit.ts` (new), `src/components/admin/CheckInCodeEntry.tsx`, `src/components/admin/CheckInResultCard.tsx`, `CLAUDE.md`, and `PROGRESS.md` changed.

- 2026-09-01 — Closed a real gap: the anonymous, not-logged-in booking path (`PATCH /api/bookings/[id]`) could re-price at member rate — and would soon have been able to spend F&B credit — for anyone entering an existing active member's real name/email/phone at Pay Now, with no authentication at all. Fixed by hardcoding `isMember: false` for that path's `priceBooking` call, regardless of the resolved `Customer`'s actual membership status; `Customer` look-up-or-create/attachment for record-keeping is unchanged, so the booking still shows in that member's account history once they log in. `PaymentStep.tsx`'s "final price changed" notice was collapsed from two member-rate/guest-fee-specific branches (the guest-fee-waived branch was now permanently dead) into one tier-agnostic "pricing was updated since you started this booking" message; `guestFeeWaived` removed from the `PriceUpdate` interface and `BookingForm.tsx`'s setter. Also fixed a pre-existing bug found along the way: `BookingSummary`'s estimate caption was a static "non-member rate" string shown identically on both flows, wrong on the member path since it always said non-member even when member pricing was shown — now threads the existing `rateTier` value through `BookingForm` → `ReviewStep`/`PaymentStep` → `BookingSummary` for an accurate caption on both. Session-aware member direct-booking (`POST /api/bookings`) is completely unchanged and unaffected — regression-confirmed member rate and full-or-nothing credit redemption still work exactly as before. No schema changes. Verified live via the Browser pane: anonymous booking with a brand-new email charged non-member rate as before (regression baseline); anonymous booking using an existing active member's real contact details charged non-member rate, left that member's `creditBalanceCentavos` and `MembershipCreditTransaction` rows untouched, still routed to PayMongo checkout instead of auto-confirming, and still correctly attached `Booking.customerId` to that member's real `Customer` row — spot-checked across a court and a golf-sim resource; a simulated mid-hold `PricingRule` edit still surfaced the new tier-agnostic notice with the correct new total; guest fee still charged (never waived) for a genuine non-member with guests; `/admin/bookings` showed the correct non-member amount and correctly attached customer for the case-2 booking; `booking_no_overlap` exclusion constraint reconfirmed via `pg_constraint`. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `git status` confirmed only `src/app/api/bookings/[id]/route.ts`, `src/components/booking/steps/PaymentStep.tsx`, `src/components/booking/steps/ReviewStep.tsx`, `src/components/booking/BookingForm.tsx`, `src/components/booking/BookingSummary.tsx`, `CLAUDE.md`, and `PROGRESS.md` changed — no scope leakage into `src/app/api/bookings/route.ts` or `src/lib/customer-resolution.ts`.

- 2026-09-01 — Three-part admin Check-In polish pass, no verification-logic changes. (1) Fixed a real race in `CheckInScanner.tsx`: the mount effect's cleanup checked `scanner.isScanning` before calling `.stop()`, but under React Strict Mode's dev-mode double-invoke the cleanup can run before the `.start()` promise resolves, so `isScanning` was still `false` and `.stop()` was skipped — leaving a live camera stream and its injected `<video>` node behind for a second `Html5Qrcode` instance to stack on top of. Rewrote it so both possible orderings (cleanup-before-resolve and resolve-before-cleanup) each call `.stop()` then `.clear()` exactly once, whichever happens later. (2) `CheckInTabs.tsx`'s `activeTab` now defaults to `null` instead of `'scan'`, so neither the camera nor the code-entry form mounts until the admin explicitly picks one — a neutral "Choose Scan or Enter Code to check in a member." prompt renders in the gap, with neither pill styled active. (3) Redesigned `CheckInResultCard.tsx` to drop the Active Member state's `green-50`/`green-200`/`green-700` classes — a real violation of CLAUDE.md's "no default Tailwind green for status badges" convention — in favor of a neutral white/gray-bordered card; all four states (active/expired/no-membership/not-found·rate-limited) now share one icon+label header layout (new `CheckCircleIcon`/`AlertTriangleIcon`/`XCircleIcon`/`InfoIcon`, added to `AdminIcons.tsx` following its existing 24×24/stroke-1.5 convention, since none of the four existed there or in the public-site `Icons.tsx`), with the expired/no-membership/error states keeping their already-compliant amber/gray/red coloring — copy unchanged throughout. Verified live via the Browser pane against a real logged-in admin (reusing `scripts/verify-admin-check-in-scan.ts`'s seed convention, plus a one-off backfill of `checkInCode` on its three seeded customers so all four result states — including rate-limited, tripped via 10 rapid lookups — could be driven through the Enter Code tab and screenshotted): confirmed neither tab renders pre-choice, Enter Code works standalone with zero `<video>` elements ever appearing, and all four/five result cards (active/expired/no-membership/not-found/rate-limited) render with the correct copy, correct icon, and — checked via a DOM regex scan of the card's `innerHTML` — zero `green-*` classes anywhere on the Active Member card. Rapid Scan↔Enter-Code switching (6 toggles) produced no console errors, no duplicate/stray `<video>` elements, and no unhandled rejections — each Strict-Mode double-mount's `.start()` failure (camera access is sandboxed/blocked in this browser automation environment, so this could only exercise the failure/teardown path, not a real live feed) was caught and handled exactly once per mount, consistent with the race fix. **Known limitation, same shape as the original 2026-08-31 camera-scan entry**: a real "exactly one live feed across a Strict-Mode remount" check needs an actual camera and can't be exercised by this browser automation — Arjay should spot-check this on a real device (rapid tab switching back to Scan, confirming only one video feed ever appears). `tsc --noEmit` clean; `git status` confirmed only `src/components/admin/CheckInScanner.tsx`, `src/components/admin/CheckInTabs.tsx`, `src/components/admin/CheckInResultCard.tsx`, `src/components/admin/AdminIcons.tsx`, and `PROGRESS.md` changed. Post-cleanup counts (8 customers / 1 membership / 2 admin users) match the pre-test baseline exactly.

- 2026-09-01 — Applied the established Navbar-legibility dark-band fix (previously `/reset-password`, `/book`, `/book/confirmation`, `/forgot-password`) to `/membership/apply`: the back-link + "Become a Member" heading/subheading are now wrapped in their own `bg-brand-dark` band (`pt-40 pb-20 md:pt-48 md:pb-28`, matching `/reset-password`/`/account`'s shape) directly below `<Navbar />`, with the back-link and heading text flipped to light-on-dark (`text-accent-light`/`text-brand-light`). The form card (`MembershipApplicationForm`) and its own container below the band are untouched — same `bg-background` wrapper, just re-padded (`pt-16 pb-16 md:pt-20`) now that the heading moved out of it. Verified live via the Browser pane: Navbar text is legible against the new dark band on first paint at `/membership/apply` (direct URL load), back-link/heading legible within the band, form fields unchanged; confirmed the Navbar's fixed-position solid-on-scroll behavior is unaffected (`position: fixed`, `bg-brand-light` on its wrapper) via a DOM check post-scroll; regression-checked `/book` and `/membership` both render correctly, confirming no shared component was altered. No known instances of the Navbar-legibility bug remain as of 2026-09-01.

- 2026-09-01 — Added a branded membership-certificate PDF (new `@react-pdf/renderer` dependency, approved exception, v4.9.0, no peer-dependency warnings) attached to the first-time membership activation email only: `src/lib/membership-certificate-pdf.tsx` renders name/tier/activation fee/F&B credit/total paid/expiry/real PayMongo payment reference/a static "Approved by Admin" label/the 3 member perks/a placeholder rules-and-regulations paragraph. `sendActivationEmail` now moves the perks list out of the email body and into the PDF only when `hasReceipt && tierName` (i.e. a genuine membership activation, never the plain-activation-link case); PDF generation failure is caught and logged, falling back to sending the email with no attachment rather than blocking activation. `sendStaffMembershipActivationEmail` gained a "PayMongo Reference" ledger row. Found and fixed a real staleness bug while wiring the webhook: the in-memory `membershipPayment` object fetched before the `$transaction` never reflects the `paymongoPaymentIntentId` value the transaction itself writes, so both new call sites use the webhook's own local `paymentIntentId` variable instead — using the stale field would have always rendered "Not available"/blank. Also found and fixed a real font bug during verification: react-pdf's built-in Helvetica has no glyph for `formatCentavos`'s ₱ sign, rendering it as a garbled overlapping character — fixed by registering Noto Sans (via its Google Fonts CDN TTF URLs) as the certificate's font instead of the built-in base-14 font. Verified end-to-end via a temporary self-signed-webhook script (removed after, same convention as `scripts/verify-membership-renewal.ts`) for all 3 tiers: real sent emails inspected via the Resend MCP (`list-emails`/`get-email`/`list-sent-email-attachments`) confirmed perks removed from the body, PDF attached, and the downloaded PDF's every field correct (including the ₱ sign rendering correctly post-fix) for 3-month/6-month/12-month; staff email's new PayMongo Reference row confirmed correct per tier. Regression-confirmed via the same method: a renewal webhook's customer + staff emails are byte-identical to before (no attachment, no reference row), and the pre-existing booking-confirmation webhook branch (exercised via `scripts/verify-staff-booking-notification.ts`) is unaffected. Rule-matching (`element.matches(rule.selectorText)`, walked recursively through `@layer` blocks) sidesteps this since it doesn't depend on paint — worth reaching for first over `getComputedStyle` on any color/style check in future sessions until this environment issue is resolved.

Open candidates for what's next (not prioritized against each other): the membership tier-activation *payment* flow (an actual PayMongo charge for the plan price, vs. today's free/manual admin approval), `middleware.ts`'s own `auth()` wrapper still having no `role === 'admin'` check (flagged follow-up for defense-in-depth on `staging`/Vercel — confirmed non-executing locally so it can't be exercised/verified here, but worth doing before `staging`/`main` promotion even though every route already has its own working check), and a member-aware booking flow (`/book` currently only supports non-member pricing — Navbar's Book a Court is hidden for logged-in members with a placeholder-only entry point on `/account` instead, per CLAUDE.md's Open/Not Yet Decided list).
- Home mobile-responsiveness fixes (per `docs/audits/2026-08-21-home-mobile-audit.md`).
- Remaining build-scope work, no particular order implied: Admin Panel (including membership application review), membership tier-activation payment flow, member-aware booking flow. (Member login, the admin-gate `role` check fix, and `/account`'s real data wiring are all now done — see the Member account bullet above.)
- Local dev query-logging & connection-pooling: **this thread is now fully closed out.** Duplicate Prisma query-log lines are fixed (listener now attaches once, inside the client-creation function), and the earlier local-dev switch to the session-mode (`:5432`) `DATABASE_URL` — already verified to meaningfully cut admin-page load latency — is now documented as a locked-in decision in both `.env.local` and CLAUDE.md's Architecture Decisions. See 2026-08-26 Completed Log entry. Nothing further queued here.
